column-gap: 18px; row-gap: 6px; font-size: 14px; margin: 0 0 28px; padding: 0; }\n  .meta-grid dt { color: var(--muted); font: 11.5px ui-monospace, monospace; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; padding-top: 2px; }\n  .meta-grid dd { margin: 0; color: var(--fg-soft); font-size: 13.5px; }\n  .meta-grid dd a { color: var(--accent); text-decoration: none; }\n  .meta-grid dd code { font: 12.5px ui-monospace, monospace; }\n\n  /* Callout — for acceptance note, walking-skeleton, backtest-gate */\n  .p-callout { border: 1px solid var(--rule); border-left: 4px solid var(--accent); background: var(--surface); border-radius: 0 10px 10px 0; padding: 16px 22px; margin: 0 0 28px; }\n  .p-callout-label { font: 700 11px/1 \"Inter\", sans-serif; letter-spacing: 0.08em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--accent); margin-bottom: 8px; }\n  .p-callout-warn { border-left-color: var(--warn); }\n  .p-callout-warn .p-callout-label { color: var(--warn); }\n  .p-callout-info { border-left-color: var(--info); }\n  .p-callout-info .p-callout-label { color: var(--info); }\n  .p-callout p { margin: 0 0 8px; font-size: 14.5px; line-height: 1.6; color: var(--fg-soft); }\n  .p-callout p:last-child { margin-bottom: 0; }\n  .p-callout strong { color: var(--fg); }\n\n  /* P13 summary strip */\n  .p-summary { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr); gap: 14px; margin: 0 0 36px; }\n  .p-summary-cell { background: var(--surface); border: 1px solid var(--rule); border-radius: 10px; padding: 16px 18px; }\n  .p-summary-k { font: 700 11px/1 \"Inter\", sans-serif; letter-spacing: 0.06em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--muted); margin-bottom: 8px; }\n  .p-summary-v { font-size: 17px; font-weight: 600; color: var(--fg); line-height: 1.3; }\n  .p-summary-v-accent { color: var(--accent); }\n  @media (max-width: 720px) { .p-summary { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); } }\n\n  /* Sections */\n  section.s { margin: 0 0 56px; scroll-margin-top: 24px; }\n  section.s > header { display: flex; align-items: baseline; gap: 14px; margin: 0 0 14px; }\n  section.s > header .ix { font: 12px ui-monospace, monospace; background: var(--rule-soft); color: var(--fg); padding: 4px 10px; border-radius: 6px; letter-spacing: 0.04em; }\n  section.s > header h2 { margin: 0; font-size: 22px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--fg); letter-spacing: -0.01em; }\n  .sec-intro { font-size: 14.5px; color: var(--muted); margin: 0 0 20px; max-width: 70ch; }\n  section.s h3.sub { font-size: 15px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--fg); margin: 28px 0 10px; letter-spacing: -0.005em; }\n  section.s p { font-size: 14.75px; line-height: 1.65; color: var(--fg-soft); margin: 0 0 14px; max-width: 72ch; }\n\n  /* Per-ticker reality check table */\n  .p-rtbl { width: 100%; border-collapse: separate; border-spacing: 0; background: var(--surface); border: 1px solid var(--rule); border-radius: 10px; overflow: hidden; margin: 14px 0 6px; font-size: 13.75px; }\n  .p-rtbl th, .p-rtbl td { padding: 11px 14px; text-align: left; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--rule); vertical-align: top; }\n  .p-rtbl tr:last-child td { border-bottom: 0; }\n  .p-rtbl th { background: var(--rule-soft); font: 700 11.5px/1 \"Inter\", sans-serif; letter-spacing: 0.05em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--fg); }\n  .p-rtbl td code { font: 12.5px ui-monospace, monospace; background: var(--rule-soft); padding: 1px 5px; border-radius: 3px; color: var(--fg); }\n\n  /* P12 rubric cards — FR / NFR */\n  .p-rubric { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); gap: 14px; margin: 16px 0; }\n  .p-rubric-card { background: var(--surface); border: 1px solid var(--rule); border-radius: 10px; padding: 16px 18px; }\n  .p-rubric-card .p-rid { display: inline-block; font: 700 11px/1 ui-monospace, monospace; padding: 4px 7px; border-radius: 5px; letter-spacing: 0.04em; background: var(--accent-soft); color: var(--accent); margin-bottom: 8px; }\n  .p-rubric-card .p-rid-nfr { background: var(--info-soft); color: var(--info); }\n  .p-rubric-card h4 { margin: 0 0 8px; font-size: 14.5px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--fg); line-height: 1.35; }\n  .p-rubric-card p { margin: 0; font-size: 13.5px; color: var(--fg-soft); line-height: 1.55; }\n  .p-rubric-card .p-fitness { margin-top: 10px; padding-top: 10px; border-top: 1px dashed var(--rule); font: 11.5px/1.5 ui-monospace, monospace; color: var(--muted); }\n  .p-rubric-card .p-fitness strong { color: var(--fg-soft); font-weight: 700; }\n  @media (max-width: 800px) { .p-rubric { grid-template-columns: 1fr; } }\n\n  /* Alternatives — Alt cards with reject/accept chevron */\n  .p-alts { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 14px; margin: 16px 0; }\n  .p-alt { background: var(--surface); border: 1px solid var(--rule); border-radius: 10px; padding: 18px 22px; position: relative; }\n  .p-alt-rejected { border-left: 4px solid var(--danger); }\n  .p-alt-chosen   { border-left: 4px solid var(--ok); background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--ok-soft) 0%, var(--surface) 14%); }\n  .p-alt h3 { margin: 0 0 4px; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--fg); display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; }\n  .p-alt-chev { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; width: 22px; height: 22px; border-radius: 50%; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 700; }\n  .p-alt-chev-rejected { background: var(--danger-soft); color: var(--danger); }\n  .p-alt-chev-chosen   { background: var(--ok-soft); color: var(--ok); }\n  .p-alt-tag { font: 700 10.5px/1 \"Inter\", sans-serif; letter-spacing: 0.08em; text-transform: uppercase; padding: 4px 8px; border-radius: 999px; margin-left: 8px; }\n  .p-alt-tag-rejected { background: var(--danger-soft); color: var(--danger); }\n  .p-alt-tag-chosen   { background: var(--ok-soft); color: var(--ok); }\n  .p-alt p { margin: 6px 0 0; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.55; color: var(--fg-soft); }\n  .p-alt-rows { display: grid; grid-template-columns: max-content 1fr; column-gap: 14px; row-gap: 4px; margin-top: 12px; font-size: 13.25px; }\n  .p-alt-rows dt { font: 700 11px/1.5 \"Inter\", sans-serif; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--muted); padding-top: 2px; }\n  .p-alt-rows dd { margin: 0; color: var(--fg-soft); line-height: 1.55; }\n\n  /* P14 SVG diagram */\n  .p-diagram { background: var(--surface); border: 1px solid var(--rule); border-radius: 12px; padding: 22px 18px 14px; margin: 22px 0; }\n  .p-diagram svg { display: block; width: 100%; height: auto; }\n  .p-diagram-caption { margin: 10px 6px 0; font-size: 12.5px; color: var(--muted); text-align: center; }\n  .p-node-rect { fill: var(--bg); stroke: var(--rule); stroke-width: 1.5; }\n  .p-node-rect-accent { fill: var(--accent-soft); stroke: var(--accent); }\n  .p-node-rect-warn { fill: var(--warn-soft); stroke: var(--warn); }\n  .p-node-title { fill: var(--fg); font: 600 12px \"Inter\", sans-serif; }\n  .p-node-sub { fill: var(--muted); font: 10.5px ui-monospace, monospace; }\n  .p-edge { stroke: var(--fg-soft); stroke-width: 1.4; fill: none; }\n  .p-edge-async { stroke: var(--accent); stroke-dasharray: 4 3; }\n  .p-edge-label { fill: var(--muted); font: 10.5px ui-monospace, monospace; }\n\n  /* P16 code panel */\n  .p-code { margin: 18px 0; border-radius: 10px; border: 1px solid var(--rule); overflow: hidden; background: var(--surface); }\n  .p-code-file { background: var(--rule-soft); padding: 8px 14px; font: 11.5px ui-monospace, monospace; color: var(--muted); letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--rule); }\n  .p-code-body { margin: 0; padding: 16px 18px; background: #14141a; color: #e8e6de; font: 12.75px/1.55 ui-monospace, monospace; overflow-x: auto; }\n  .p-code-body .tok-kw  { color: #c792ea; }\n  .p-code-body .tok-str { color: #c3e88d; }\n  .p-code-body .tok-cm  { color: #6a7480; font-style: italic; }\n  .p-code-body .tok-fn  { color: #82aaff; }\n\n  /* Sequencing — Week table */\n  .p-seq { border: 1px solid var(--rule); border-radius: 12px; overflow: hidden; background: var(--surface); margin: 16px 0; }\n  .p-seq-row { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 110px 1fr 1fr 1fr; }\n  .p-seq-row + .p-seq-row { border-top: 1px solid var(--rule); }\n  .p-seq-row > div { padding: 14px 16px; font-size: 13.5px; line-height: 1.55; color: var(--fg-soft); border-left: 1px solid var(--rule); }\n  .p-seq-row > div:first-child { border-left: 0; }\n  .p-seq-row-head > div { background: var(--rule-soft); color: var(--fg); font: 700 11.5px/1 \"Inter\", sans-serif; letter-spacing: 0.05em; text-transform: uppercase; padding: 12px 16px; }\n  .p-seq-week { font: 700 13px ui-monospace, monospace; color: var(--accent); letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; }\n  .p-seq-slip { color: var(--warn); font-style: italic; }\n\n  /* Failure-modes table */\n  .p-fmtbl { width: 100%; border-collapse: separate; border-spacing: 0; background: var(--surface); border: 1px solid var(--rule); border-radius: 10px; overflow: hidden; margin: 14px 0; font-size: 13.5px; }\n  .p-fmtbl th, .p-fmtbl td { padding: 11px 14px; text-align: left; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--rule); vertical-align: top; }\n  .p-fmtbl tr:last-child td { border-bottom: 0; }\n  .p-fmtbl th { background: var(--rule-soft); font: 700 11.5px/1 \"Inter\", sans-serif; letter-spacing: 0.05em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--fg); }\n  .p-fmtbl td { color: var(--fg-soft); line-height: 1.55; }\n\n  /* P5 open-questions cards */\n  .p-questions { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 14px; margin: 16px 0; max-width: 820px; }\n  .p-q { background: var(--surface); border: 1px solid var(--rule); border-left: 4px solid var(--accent); border-radius: 10px; padding: 16px 20px; }\n  .p-q-resolved { border-left-color: var(--ok); }\n  .p-q-title { font-weight: 600; font-size: 14.75px; color: var(--fg); margin-bottom: 6px; display: flex; align-items: baseline; gap: 10px; }\n  .p-q-id { font: 700 11px ui-monospace, monospace; color: var(--accent); letter-spacing: 0.04em; }\n  .p-q-id-resolved { color: var(--ok); }\n  .p-q-body { font-size: 13.75px; color: var(--fg-soft); line-height: 1.55; }\n  .p-q-body code { font-size: 0.92em; padding: 1px 5px; background: var(--rule-soft); border-radius: 3px; }\n  .p-q-meta { margin-top: 8px; font: 11.5px ui-monospace, monospace; color: var(--muted); display: flex; gap: 18px; }\n\n  /* Plain bullet lists in sections */\n  section.s ul.plain { padding-left: 20px; margin: 8px 0 14px; }\n  section.s ul.plain li { font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.6; color: var(--fg-soft); margin: 4px 0; max-width: 72ch; }\n\n  /* Footer */\n  footer { border-top: 1px solid var(--rule); padding-top: 20px; margin-top: 60px; font-size: 13px; color: var(--muted); }\n  footer code { font: 12px ui-monospace, monospace; }\n\n  /* Inline code in prose */\n  p code, li code, td code { font-size: 0.92em; padding: 1px 5px; background: var(--rule-soft); border-radius: 3px; color: var(--fg); }\n  /* Links */\n  main a { color: var(--accent); text-decoration: underline; text-decoration-thickness: 1px; text-underline-offset: 3px; text-decoration-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 45%, transparent); }\n  main a:hover { text-decoration-color: var(--accent); }\n\n  @media (max-width: 1000px) {\n    .shell { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }\n    aside { display: none; }\n    main { padding: 28px 24px 80px; }\n    .p-seq-row { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }\n  }\n</style>\n</head>\n<body>\n<div class=\"shell\">\n\n  <aside>\n    <div class=\"brand\">\n      <div class=\"brand-dot\"></div>\n      <div>\n        <div class=\"brand-name\">stock-review</div>\n        <div class=\"brand-kind\">Design doc</div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n    <div class=\"nav-kicker\">Sections</div>\n    <ol class=\"nav\">\n      <li><a href=\"#problem\">Problem</a></li>\n      <li><a href=\"#context\">Context</a></li>\n      <li><a href=\"#constraints\">Constraints</a></li>\n      <li><a href=\"#alternatives\">Alternatives</a></li>\n      <li><a href=\"#recommended\">Recommended approach</a></li>\n      <li><a href=\"#consequences\">Consequences</a></li>\n      <li><a href=\"#sequencing\">Sequencing &amp; appetite</a></li>\n      <li><a href=\"#operability\">Operability</a></li>\n      <li><a href=\"#open\">Open questions</a></li>\n    </ol>\n    <div class=\"status-block\">\n      <div class=\"lbl\">Status</div>\n      <div class=\"val\">Accepted · 2026-05-17</div>\n      <div class=\"lbl\" style=\"margin-top:12px\">Linear</div>\n      <div class=\"val\"><a href=\"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-66\" style=\"color:var(--accent); text-decoration:none\">ABA-66</a></div>\n    </div>\n  </aside>\n\n  <main>\n\n    <header class=\"doc-head\">\n      <div class=\"breadcrumbs\">docs / design-docs / engagement-kpi-enrichment / design-doc.md</div>\n      <h1>Engagement-KPI enrichment for <code style=\"font-size:0.78em; background:var(--rule-soft); padding:2px 8px; border-radius:6px;\">/stock-model</code></h1>\n      <p class=\"lede\">Wire the most recent quarter's engagement KPIs (DAP, DAUq, paid clicks) into <code>/stock-model</code> as a bounded, audit-trailed Year-1 anchor modifier — so post-earnings DCFs reflect actual engagement trajectory rather than weeks-stale sell-side consensus.</p>\n      <div class=\"pill-row\">\n        <span class=\"pill pill-accepted\"><span class=\"dot\"></span>Accepted</span>\n        <span class=\"pill pill-appetite\">Appetite · 3 weeks (fixed)</span>\n        <span class=\"pill pill-meta\">APPLICATION / INCUMBENT only</span>\n        <span class=\"pill pill-meta\">8 FRs · 7 NFRs · 7 OQs</span>\n      </div>\n      <dl class=\"meta-grid\">\n        <dt>authors</dt>   <dd>Anton Babushkin</dd>\n        <dt>created</dt>   <dd>2026-05-14</dd>\n        <dt>updated</dt>   <dd>2026-05-17</dd>\n        <dt>supersedes</dt><dd>none</dd>\n        <dt>linear</dt>    <dd><a href=\"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-66\">ABA-66</a></dd>\n        <dt>plan review</dt><dd><a href=\"../../plan-reviews/engagement-kpi-enrichment/review.md\">REVISE → six conditions addressed in this revision</a></dd>\n        <dt>spike decision</dt><dd><a href=\"./spike-decision.md\">RESHAPE → PROCEED (supersedes the \"lead-prediction\" framing)</a></dd>\n      </dl>\n    </header>\n\n    <!-- Acceptance note callout -->\n    <div class=\"p-callout p-callout-warn\">\n      <div class=\"p-callout-label\">Acceptance note · 2026-05-17</div>\n      <p>Accepted with the spike-decision pivot in force. The doc body below frames the modifier as a <strong>lead</strong> signal — engagement direction <em>predicts</em> the consensus revision before it lands, and NFR7's backtest is the gate.</p>\n      <p>The Task 7 spike showed the wayback data source needed for that gate is not viable, and reshaped the philosophy to <strong>confirm/lag</strong>: engagement direction agrees with (or contradicts) the revision that has already started, measured against Yahoo Finance's live <code>/analysis/</code> EPS Trend \"Current vs 30 Days Ago\" table.</p>\n      <p>Where the body below says \"lead\", \"predict\", or \"consensus revision direction\" in the context of NFR7 / backtest gating, read it as superseded by <a href=\"./spike-decision.md\">spike-decision.md</a>. All other design choices — EDGAR-anchored extraction, pre-registered constants, two-cap audit trail, base-scenario-only application, KPI-map versioning — survive the pivot unchanged.</p>\n    </div>\n\n    <!-- Summary strip -->\n    <div class=\"p-summary\">\n      <div class=\"p-summary-cell\"><div class=\"p-summary-k\">Status</div><div class=\"p-summary-v\">Accepted</div></div>\n      <div class=\"p-summary-cell\"><div class=\"p-summary-k\">Appetite</div><div class=\"p-summary-v p-summary-v-accent\">3 weeks · fixed cap</div></div>\n      <div class=\"p-summary-cell\"><div class=\"p-summary-k\">Scope (AI layers)</div><div class=\"p-summary-v\">APPLICATION · INCUMBENT</div></div>\n      <div class=\"p-summary-cell\"><div class=\"p-summary-k\">Seed tickers</div><div class=\"p-summary-v\">META · NFLX · RDDT · GOOGL</div></div>\n    </div>\n\n    <!-- 1. Problem -->\n    <section class=\"s\" id=\"problem\">\n      <header><span class=\"ix\">01</span><h2>Problem</h2></header>\n      <p>When <code>/stock-model</code> runs for ad-driven and subscription companies (APPLICATION and INCUMBENT layers — META, NFLX, RDDT, GOOGL), the DCF's Year-1 revenue and FCF-margin inputs anchor on <code>ntm_revenue</code> from <code>yfinance.get_estimates</code>. That figure is a single consensus number compiled by sell-side analysts. By the time it lands in yfinance it is already <strong>1–8 weeks stale</strong> relative to the latest earnings release.</p>\n      <p>For these business models, the most recent quarter's engagement metrics (DAU, ARPU, paid-sub net adds, impressions, CPM) are the <em>leading</em> indicator of next-quarter and next-year revenue. The current skill is structurally blind to them — it can see what the analyst median was last week, but not the post-print engagement trajectory the analyst median will be updated <em>to</em> over the coming weeks.</p>\n      <h3 class=\"sub\">Who's affected</h3>\n      <p>The user running <code>/stock-model META</code> (or similar) within ~6 weeks of an earnings release. Today the bear/base/bull scenarios are perturbations around a stale anchor with no signal about whether the trajectory is accelerating or decelerating versus what the consensus assumes.</p>\n    </section>\n\n    <!-- 2. Context -->\n    <section class=\"s\" id=\"context\">\n      <header><span class=\"ix\">02</span><h2>Context</h2></header>\n\n      <h3 class=\"sub\">Prior work and skill structure</h3>\n      <ul class=\"plain\">\n        <li><code>/stock-model</code> is the v1.6 skill specified in <code>skills/stock-model/SKILL.md</code>. Year-1 math is reconciled across two paths (ESTABLISHED two-stage DCF, EMERGING pre-profit variant); both use <code>ntm_revenue</code> as the anchor.</li>\n        <li>The Manual Input Protocol (<code>skills/_shared/MANUAL_INPUT_PROTOCOL.md</code>) is the established pattern for inputs that cannot be derived from MCP data. Web-search results flow through it for user confirmation — never silently committed.</li>\n        <li>The AI-layer classification (INFRASTRUCTURE / FOUNDATION / APPLICATION / INCUMBENT / NONE) is produced upstream by <code>/stock-signal</code> and lives in <code>stages.signal.ai_layer</code>.</li>\n        <li>Confidence caps (HIGH / MEDIUM / LOW) already cascade across stages. Any EDGAR-anchored modifier integrates into this rather than introducing a new dimension.</li>\n      </ul>\n\n      <h3 class=\"sub\">What the ticket gets wrong, that this doc reconciles</h3>\n      <p>The ticket frames the modifier as \"positive momentum → use upper range of NTM estimate.\" The skill does not currently expose a high/low consensus band — <code>get_estimates</code> returns a single <code>ntm_revenue</code> mean. So either (a) the modifier perturbs the existing single NTM number, (b) the skill grows a high/low band, or (c) the modifier operates on a different lever entirely (FCF margin, scenario weights). Resolved in <a href=\"#alternatives\">Alternatives</a> below.</p>\n\n      <h3 class=\"sub\">Engagement-KPI reality check (per-ticker)</h3>\n      <p>A quick reality check of the ticket's KPI table against current (2026) disclosures:</p>\n      <table class=\"p-rtbl\">\n        <thead><tr><th style=\"width:90px\">Company</th><th>Ticket's primary KPI</th><th>Actually disclosed in press release?</th></tr></thead>\n        <tbody>\n          <tr><td><code>META</code></td><td>DAU/MAU, impressions</td><td><strong>DAP</strong> (\"Daily Active People\") — replaced DAU/MAU in 2023</td></tr>\n          <tr><td><code>NFLX</code></td><td>Paid-sub net adds</td><td>Stopped quarterly disclosure in Q1 2025 — annual only</td></tr>\n          <tr><td><code>RDDT</code></td><td>DAU</td><td><strong>DAUq</strong> (\"Daily Active Uniques\") — yes, quarterly</td></tr>\n          <tr><td><code>GOOGL</code></td><td>Search CPM / impression vol.</td><td>Not disclosed; only \"paid clicks\" + \"cost-per-click\" YoY % available</td></tr>\n          <tr><td><code>XYZ</code></td><td>Cash App MAUs, inflows-per-active</td><td>Cash App MAUs + gross profit per active disclosed quarterly</td></tr>\n        </tbody>\n      </table>\n      <p style=\"font-size:13.5px; color:var(--muted); margin-top:8px;\">Takeaway: the KPI map must be authored as part of this work and verified against current 10-Q / press-release language, not lifted from the ticket as gospel.</p>\n    </section>\n\n    <!-- 3. Constraints -->\n    <section class=\"s\" id=\"constraints\">\n      <header><span class=\"ix\">03</span><h2>Constraints</h2></header>\n      <p class=\"sec-intro\">Functional and non-functional requirements. Each NFR carries a named fitness function — a measurable check that fails CI loudly when violated.</p>\n\n      <h3 class=\"sub\">Functional requirements</h3>\n      <div class=\"p-rubric\">\n        <div class=\"p-rubric-card\"><span class=\"p-rid\">FR1</span><h4>AI-layer gate</h4><p>Modifier fires only when <code>upstream_signal.ai_layer ∈ {APPLICATION, INCUMBENT}</code>. The skill never second-guesses Signal — user re-runs Signal if classification is wrong.</p></div>\n        <div class=\"p-rubric-card\"><span class=\"p-rid\">FR2</span><h4>Surface via Manual Input Protocol</h4><p>Extracted KPIs and source URL are surfaced via existing MIP. Never silently applied.</p></div>\n        <div class=\"p-rubric-card\"><span class=\"p-rid\">FR3</span><h4>Direction × magnitude</h4><p>Produces direction <code>∈ {+1, 0, −1}</code> (accelerating / neutral / decelerating) and magnitude band (<code>mild</code> / <code>strong</code>).</p></div>\n        <div class=\"p-rubric-card\"><span class=\"p-rid\">FR4</span><h4>Two-cap bounding</h4><p>(a) <strong>Input cap:</strong> ±4% on Y1 anchor (base scenario only). (b) <strong>Output cap:</strong> ≤5% on base IV post-application. If the input cap alone overshoots, the multiplier is clamped further and marked <code>clamped_from</code>.</p></div>\n        <div class=\"p-rubric-card\"><span class=\"p-rid\">FR5</span><h4>Fail-safe on unavailable</h4><p>When extraction fails (no recent print, source unreachable, user rejects), records <code>engagement_modifier.status: \"unavailable\"</code> — never fabricates a direction.</p></div>\n        <div class=\"p-rubric-card\"><span class=\"p-rid\">FR6</span><h4>EMERGING never triggers</h4><p>Skipped on <code>--pre-profit</code> runs and on EMERGING profile where the company has no meaningful engagement KPI.</p></div>\n        <div class=\"p-rubric-card\"><span class=\"p-rid\">FR7</span><h4>Versioned KPI map</h4><p>Lives at <code>skills/_shared/engagement_kpi_map.json</code> with top-level <code>schema_version</code> and sibling <code>CHANGELOG.md</code>. Tickers not mapped skip with <code>status: \"no_kpi_mapping\"</code>.</p></div>\n        <div class=\"p-rubric-card\"><span class=\"p-rid\">FR8</span><h4>EDGAR-anchored sourcing</h4><p>8-K Exhibit 99.1 is the primary source. Resolution order: EDGAR MCP → direct EDGAR HTTP → <code>WebSearch</code> fallback (tagged <code>source_type: \"websearch_fallback\"</code>).</p></div>\n      </div>\n\n      <h3 class=\"sub\">Non-functional requirements (with fitness functions)</h3>\n      <div class=\"p-rubric\">\n        <div class=\"p-rubric-card\"><span class=\"p-rid p-rid-nfr\">NFR1</span><h4>Web-search latency p95 ≤ 12 s</h4><p>Per ticker.</p><div class=\"p-fitness\"><strong>Fitness:</strong> <code>tests/smoke/engagement_kpi_latency.py</code> — 5-ticker rotation; CI red above bound.</div></div>\n        <div class=\"p-rubric-card\"><span class=\"p-rid p-rid-nfr\">NFR2</span><h4>Extraction precision ≥ 80% on seed set</h4><p>Extracted KPI value and YoY match human-verified 8-K Ex 99.1 figures.</p><div class=\"p-fitness\"><strong>Fitness:</strong> <code>tests/golden/engagement_kpi_extraction.py</code> — frozen fixtures; ±2% numerical, exact-match direction.</div></div>\n        <div class=\"p-rubric-card\"><span class=\"p-rid p-rid-nfr\">NFR3</span><h4>Zero silent application</h4><p>Every applied entry has all 8 applied-fields; every non-applied entry populates <code>status_reason</code> from the enum.</p><div class=\"p-fitness\"><strong>Fitness:</strong> JSON-schema test.</div></div>\n        <div class=\"p-rubric-card\"><span class=\"p-rid p-rid-nfr\">NFR4</span><h4>Modifier impact bounded</h4><p>No scenario IV moves &gt;5% from pre-modifier value due to the engagement modifier alone.</p><div class=\"p-fitness\"><strong>Fitness:</strong> unit test asserts <code>abs(iv_with / iv_without − 1) ≤ 0.05</code>.</div></div>\n        <div class=\"p-rubric-card\"><span class=\"p-rid p-rid-nfr\">NFR5</span><h4>Confidence cap honoured</h4><p>Any <code>applied</code> run caps <code>meta.confidence</code> at MEDIUM.</p><div class=\"p-fitness\"><strong>Fitness:</strong> unit test on confidence-resolution.</div></div>\n        <div class=\"p-rubric-card\"><span class=\"p-rid p-rid-nfr\">NFR6</span><h4>Cost envelope ≤ 8k tokens</h4><p>Extraction prompt + press-release excerpt per invocation.</p><div class=\"p-fitness\"><strong>Fitness:</strong> smoke test against frozen extraction prompt + per-surface fixture.</div></div>\n        <div class=\"p-rubric-card\"><span class=\"p-rid p-rid-nfr\">NFR7</span><h4>Direction agreement ≥ 60% on n ≥ 24</h4><p><strong>Outcome metric.</strong> Over a rolling 8-quarter backtest, modifier direction agrees with actual analyst-consensus revision direction over the 4-week post-print window on ≥ 60% of ticker-quarters. Below this the modifier is signal-dressed noise and must not ship. <em>(Reshaped post-spike — see acceptance note.)</em></p><div class=\"p-fitness\"><strong>Fitness:</strong> <code>tests/backtest/engagement_kpi_revision_agreement.py</code> — frozen fixtures; n ≥ 24.</div></div>\n      </div>\n    </section>\n\n    <!-- 4. Alternatives -->\n    <section class=\"s\" id=\"alternatives\">\n      <header><span class=\"ix\">04</span><h2>Alternatives considered</h2></header>\n      <div class=\"p-alts\">\n\n        <div class=\"p-alt p-alt-rejected\">\n          <h3><span class=\"p-alt-chev p-alt-chev-rejected\">✗</span>Alt A — Do nothing (status quo)<span class=\"p-alt-tag p-alt-tag-rejected\">Rejected</span></h3>\n          <p><code>/stock-model</code> continues anchoring Year-1 inputs on stale consensus only.</p>\n          <dl class=\"p-alt-rows\">\n            <dt>blast radius</dt><dd>Continued loss of leading-indicator signal on the four highest-traffic tickers.</dd>\n            <dt>reversal cost</dt><dd>None — this is the current state.</dd>\n            <dt>why rejected</dt><dd>Concedes the most valuable enrichment for the most-used skill on the most-watched tickers.</dd>\n          </dl>\n        </div>\n\n        <div class=\"p-alt p-alt-chosen\">\n          <h3><span class=\"p-alt-chev p-alt-chev-chosen\">✓</span>Alt B — EDGAR-anchored KPI extraction with WebSearch fallback<span class=\"p-alt-tag p-alt-tag-chosen\">Chosen</span></h3>\n          <p>Optional GATHER step for APPLICATION/INCUMBENT tickers. Fetch the latest 8-K Ex 99.1 from EDGAR (deterministic URL, time-stamped); fall back to WebSearch only when no Q-end exhibit exists in the last 90 days. Extract the mapped KPI(s), compute YoY trend, apply a bounded multiplicative modifier to Y1 in the <strong>base scenario only</strong>. Bear/bull unmodified to preserve scenario-axis independence. Surfaced via MIP before commitment.</p>\n          <dl class=\"p-alt-rows\">\n            <dt>blast radius</dt><dd>Bad extraction propagates into base IV by ≤ 5% (NFR4). MIP gate gives the user a chance to reject before commit.</dd>\n            <dt>reversal cost</dt><dd>Low — disable per-run via <code>--no-engagement-modifier</code>; single-commit revert removes the feature.</dd>\n          </dl>\n        </div>\n\n        <div class=\"p-alt p-alt-rejected\">\n          <h3><span class=\"p-alt-chev p-alt-chev-rejected\">✗</span>Alt C — Grow <code>get_estimates</code> to expose high/low band<span class=\"p-alt-tag p-alt-tag-rejected\">Rejected</span></h3>\n          <p>The ticket's literal framing: surface yfinance's <code>recommendationKey</code> / analyst-range fields and shift within them.</p>\n          <dl class=\"p-alt-rows\">\n            <dt>blast radius</dt><dd>Touches the yf MCP server, not just the skill. New field across all <code>get_estimates</code> consumers.</dd>\n            <dt>reversal cost</dt><dd>High — downstream consumers depend on it once exposed.</dd>\n            <dt>why rejected</dt><dd>The analyst high/low range is itself stale; doesn't solve post-print freshness. Doesn't use the engagement signal — wrong layer.</dd>\n          </dl>\n        </div>\n\n        <div class=\"p-alt p-alt-rejected\">\n          <h3><span class=\"p-alt-chev p-alt-chev-rejected\">✗</span>Alt D — Subscribe to structured KPI feed (Visible Alpha / Bloomberg)<span class=\"p-alt-tag p-alt-tag-rejected\">Rejected</span></h3>\n          <p>Buy structured engagement data rather than scraping press releases.</p>\n          <dl class=\"p-alt-rows\">\n            <dt>blast radius</dt><dd>Paid vendor dependency, contract overhead, new MCP server.</dd>\n            <dt>reversal cost</dt><dd>Medium-high — vendor lock-in.</dd>\n            <dt>why rejected</dt><dd>Boring-technology principle inverted. User base is one. $500/mo feed is wrong shape; web-search costs cents per call and failure mode is bounded by NFR4 + MIP.</dd>\n          </dl>\n        </div>\n\n      </div>\n    </section>\n\n    <!-- 5. Recommended approach -->\n    <section class=\"s\" id=\"recommended\">\n      <header><span class=\"ix\">05</span><h2>Recommended approach</h2></header>\n      <p>Alt B with the GATHER-step flow shown below — inserted after <code>get_estimates</code> and before COMPUTE on both ESTABLISHED and EMERGING paths, conditional on AI layer + KPI-map presence.</p>\n\n      <div class=\"p-diagram\">\n        <svg viewBox=\"0 0 760 280\" role=\"img\" aria-label=\"GATHER step flow — Signal upstream feeds AI-layer gate, then KPI-map lookup, then EDGAR 8-K Ex 99.1 fetch with WebSearch fallback, then in-runtime extraction, then MIP confirmation, then modifier application to base Y1 anchor only.\">\n          <defs>\n            <marker id=\"arrow\" viewBox=\"0 0 10 10\" refX=\"9\" refY=\"5\" markerWidth=\"7\" markerHeight=\"7\" orient=\"auto\">\n              <path d=\"M0,0 L10,5 L0,10 z\" fill=\"var(--fg-soft)\"/>\n            </marker>\n            <marker id=\"arrow-accent\" viewBox=\"0 0 10 10\" refX=\"9\" refY=\"5\" markerWidth=\"7\" markerHeight=\"7\" orient=\"auto\">\n              <path d=\"M0,0 L10,5 L0,10 z\" fill=\"var(--accent)\"/>\n            </marker>\n          </defs>\n\n          <!-- Row 1: Signal → AI-layer gate → KPI-map lookup -->\n          <g><rect class=\"p-node-rect\" x=\"10\" y=\"20\" width=\"140\" height=\"58\" rx=\"8\"/>\n             <text class=\"p-node-title\" x=\"80\" y=\"44\" text-anchor=\"middle\">/stock-signal</text>\n             <text class=\"p-node-sub\" x=\"80\" y=\"62\" text-anchor=\"middle\">ai_layer</text></g>\n\n          <g><rect class=\"p-node-rect p-node-rect-accent\" x=\"200\" y=\"20\" width=\"160\" height=\"58\" rx=\"8\"/>\n             <text class=\"p-node-title\" x=\"280\" y=\"44\" text-anchor=\"middle\">AI-layer gate</text>\n             <text class=\"p-node-sub\" x=\"280\" y=\"62\" text-anchor=\"middle\">APPLICATION | INCUMBENT</text></g>\n\n          <g><rect class=\"p-node-rect p-node-rect-accent\" x=\"410\" y=\"20\" width=\"160\" height=\"58\" rx=\"8\"/>\n             <text class=\"p-node-title\" x=\"490\" y=\"44\" text-anchor=\"middle\">KPI-map lookup</text>\n             <text class=\"p-node-sub\" x=\"490\" y=\"62\" text-anchor=\"middle\">engagement_kpi_map.json</text></g>\n\n          <g><rect class=\"p-node-rect p-node-rect-warn\" x=\"620\" y=\"20\" width=\"130\" height=\"58\" rx=\"8\"/>\n             <text class=\"p-node-title\" x=\"685\" y=\"44\" text-anchor=\"middle\">status: unavailable</text>\n             <text class=\"p-node-sub\" x=\"685\" y=\"62\" text-anchor=\"middle\">skip · no fabrication</text></g>\n\n          <!-- Edges row 1 -->\n          <path class=\"p-edge\" d=\"M150 49 L200 49\" marker-end=\"url(#arrow)\"/>\n          <path class=\"p-edge\" d=\"M360 49 L410 49\" marker-end=\"url(#arrow)\"/>\n          <path class=\"p-edge p-edge-async\" d=\"M280 78 L280 110 L685 110 L685 78\" marker-end=\"url(#arrow-accent)\"/>\n          <text class=\"p-edge-label\" x=\"450\" y=\"105\" text-anchor=\"middle\">non-qualifying / no mapping</text>\n\n          <!-- Row 2: EDGAR fetch → Extract → MIP confirm → Apply -->\n          <g><rect class=\"p-node-rect\" x=\"10\" y=\"160\" width=\"160\" height=\"58\" rx=\"8\"/>\n             <text class=\"p-node-title\" x=\"90\" y=\"184\" text-anchor=\"middle\">EDGAR 8-K Ex 99.1</text>\n             <text class=\"p-node-sub\" x=\"90\" y=\"202\" text-anchor=\"middle\">MCP → HTTP → WebSearch</text></g>\n\n          <g><rect class=\"p-node-rect\" x=\"210\" y=\"160\" width=\"160\" height=\"58\" rx=\"8\"/>\n             <text class=\"p-node-title\" x=\"290\" y=\"184\" text-anchor=\"middle\">In-runtime extract</text>\n             <text class=\"p-node-sub\" x=\"290\" y=\"202\" text-anchor=\"middle\">kpi_value · yoy · period</text></g>\n\n          <g><rect class=\"p-node-rect p-node-rect-accent\" x=\"410\" y=\"160\" width=\"160\" height=\"58\" rx=\"8\"/>\n             <text class=\"p-node-title\" x=\"490\" y=\"184\" text-anchor=\"middle\">MIP confirm</text>\n             <text class=\"p-node-sub\" x=\"490\" y=\"202\" text-anchor=\"middle\">y · n · paste-correction</text></g>\n\n          <g><rect class=\"p-node-rect p-node-rect-accent\" x=\"610\" y=\"160\" width=\"140\" height=\"58\" rx=\"8\"/>\n             <text class=\"p-node-title\" x=\"680\" y=\"184\" text-anchor=\"middle\">Apply modifier</text>\n             <text class=\"p-node-sub\" x=\"680\" y=\"202\" text-anchor=\"middle\">base Y1 anchor only</text></g>\n\n          <!-- Down-edge from KPI-map to EDGAR -->\n          <path class=\"p-edge\" d=\"M490 78 L490 130 L90 130 L90 160\" marker-end=\"url(#arrow)\"/>\n          <text class=\"p-edge-label\" x=\"285\" y=\"124\" text-anchor=\"middle\">mapped → fetch</text>\n\n          <!-- Edges row 2 -->\n          <path class=\"p-edge\" d=\"M170 189 L210 189\" marker-end=\"url(#arrow)\"/>\n          <path class=\"p-edge\" d=\"M370 189 L410 189\" marker-end=\"url(#arrow)\"/>\n          <path class=\"p-edge\" d=\"M570 189 L610 189\" marker-end=\"url(#arrow)\"/>\n\n          <!-- Reject edge from MIP back to unavailable -->\n          <path class=\"p-edge p-edge-async\" d=\"M490 218 L490 252 L685 252 L685 78\" marker-end=\"url(#arrow-accent)\"/>\n          <text class=\"p-edge-label\" x=\"588\" y=\"246\" text-anchor=\"middle\">user skips · extraction fails</text>\n        </svg>\n        <div class=\"p-diagram-caption\">GATHER-step flow — solid edges are the happy path; dashed accent edges are skip/unavailable routes. Every terminal state writes an audit-trail entry.</div>\n      </div>\n\n      <h3 class=\"sub\">JSON contract — new optional block under <code>stages.model</code></h3>\n      <div class=\"p-code\">\n        <div class=\"p-code-file\">stages.model.engagement_modifier</div>\n        <pre class=\"p-code-body\"><span class=\"tok-cm\">// Emitted by every invocation when --engagement-modifier is set.</span>\n<span class=\"tok-cm\">// Non-applied states populate status_reason from the documented enum.</span>\n{\n  <span class=\"tok-str\">\"status\"</span>:            <span class=\"tok-str\">\"applied | unavailable | no_kpi_mapping | user_skipped\"</span>,\n  <span class=\"tok-str\">\"status_reason\"</span>:     <span class=\"tok-str\">\"missing_ai_layer | no_recent_print | source_unreachable | extraction_failed | non_interactive | null\"</span>,\n  <span class=\"tok-str\">\"kpi_name\"</span>:          <span class=\"tok-str\">\"DAP\"</span>,\n  <span class=\"tok-str\">\"kpi_value\"</span>:         <span class=\"tok-fn\">3.43</span>,\n  <span class=\"tok-str\">\"kpi_unit\"</span>:          <span class=\"tok-str\">\"billion\"</span>,\n  <span class=\"tok-str\">\"kpi_period\"</span>:        <span class=\"tok-str\">\"Q1 2026\"</span>,\n  <span class=\"tok-str\">\"yoy_change\"</span>:        <span class=\"tok-fn\">0.061</span>,\n  <span class=\"tok-str\">\"direction\"</span>:         <span class=\"tok-fn\">1</span>,         <span class=\"tok-cm\">// +1 / 0 / -1</span>\n  <span class=\"tok-str\">\"magnitude\"</span>:         <span class=\"tok-str\">\"mild\"</span>,    <span class=\"tok-cm\">// mild | strong</span>\n  <span class=\"tok-str\">\"base_anchor_multiplier\"</span>: <span class=\"tok-fn\">1.02</span>, <span class=\"tok-cm\">// input cap ±4%; clamped further if output cap (≤5%) bites</span>\n  <span class=\"tok-str\">\"source_url\"</span>:        <span class=\"tok-str\">\"https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1326801/...\"</span>,\n  <span class=\"tok-str\">\"user_confirmed\"</span>:    <span class=\"tok-kw\">true</span>\n}</pre>\n      </div>\n\n      <h3 class=\"sub\">OUTPUT line under the base-scenario block</h3>\n      <div class=\"p-code\">\n        <div class=\"p-code-file\">stdout · base scenario</div>\n        <pre class=\"p-code-body\">Engagement modifier (base only): DAP <span class=\"tok-fn\">+6.1%</span> YoY (Q1 2026) → <span class=\"tok-fn\">+2%</span> anchor uplift (<span class=\"tok-str\">mild positive</span>, user-confirmed)\n  Source: https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1326801/000162828026028364/...</pre>\n      </div>\n\n      <h3 class=\"sub\">CLI flag</h3>\n      <p><code>--no-engagement-modifier</code> skips the GATHER step entirely. Useful for reproducibility and for cases where the user wants the unmodified DCF. <em>(Post-spike-pivot: behaviour is opt-in via <code>--engagement-modifier</code>; default is off until n ≥ 24 forward samples accumulate.)</em></p>\n    </section>\n\n    <!-- 6. Consequences -->\n    <section class=\"s\" id=\"consequences\">\n      <header><span class=\"ix\">06</span><h2>Consequences</h2></header>\n\n      <h3 class=\"sub\">Positive</h3>\n      <ul class=\"plain\">\n        <li>Post-earnings runs reflect the latest engagement trajectory rather than stale consensus.</li>\n        <li>Audit trail (source URL + user confirmation) lets the user verify or replay the call.</li>\n        <li>Pattern is generalisable — segment-revenue enrichment can re-use the GATHER infrastructure.</li>\n      </ul>\n\n      <h3 class=\"sub\">Negative</h3>\n      <ul class=\"plain\">\n        <li>EDGAR-anchoring largely removes URL non-determinism. Residual risk is on the WebSearch fallback path, tagged <code>source_type: \"websearch_fallback\"</code> in the audit trail.</li>\n        <li>LLM extraction will sometimes fail — typically silently producing a plausible-but-wrong number. NFR2 + the MIP gate are the two-tier defence; both required.</li>\n        <li>KPI map is a maintenance burden — when META rebrands DAP, the map must be updated. See OQ4 (resolved → weekly drift CI).</li>\n      </ul>\n\n      <div class=\"p-callout p-callout-warn\" style=\"margin-top:22px\">\n        <div class=\"p-callout-label\">Walking-skeleton requirement</div>\n        <p><strong>Yes — required.</strong> Before scoping the full implementation, build an end-to-end spike across <strong>three surface types</strong> (not one): META Q1 2026 (PDF), RDDT Q1 2026 (HTML), NFLX Q1 2026 (shareholder letter). One ticker each, one mapped KPI each, one extraction-prompt iteration allowed, modifier application + MIP confirmation + JSON write end-to-end.</p>\n        <p>Goals: validate ≥80% extraction precision (NFR2) on <em>each</em> of the three surfaces; ±4% base-IV cap holds (NFR4); in-session token cost bounded (NFR6). <strong>Failing any one surface stops the project</strong> — pivot to Alt D or reshape KPI map.</p>\n      </div>\n\n      <div class=\"p-callout p-callout-info\">\n        <div class=\"p-callout-label\">Backtest gate (precondition to merge)</div>\n        <p>Independent of the skeleton, the load-bearing assumption — \"YoY engagement leads analyst consensus revision direction\" — must be backtested before the feature ships. Up to <strong>40 candidate ticker-quarters</strong> (5 seed × 8 historical). <strong>Minimum valid sample: 32</strong>; NFR7's hard floor is <strong>24</strong>.</p>\n        <p>Pass condition: hit rate ≥ 60% (NFR7). Below this the modifier ships disabled by default — flag becomes <code>--engagement-modifier</code> (opt-in) rather than <code>--no-engagement-modifier</code> (opt-out). Fixture-frozen so future map / formula changes can be re-evaluated against the same baseline.</p>\n      </div>\n\n      <h3 class=\"sub\">ADR commitments</h3>\n      <div class=\"p-rubric\">\n        <div class=\"p-rubric-card\"><span class=\"p-rid\">ADR-1</span><h4><code>engagement-modifier-constants.md</code></h4><p>Deadband (±2% YoY), strong-magnitude threshold (8%), base-only application, ±4% cap, AND the 60% backtest-pass threshold with its justification (coin-flip + ≥10pp lift). <strong>Pre-registered</strong> — committed before the Week 2 backtest is run. One in-sample revision allowed; further revisions require fresh data.</p></div>\n        <div class=\"p-rubric-card\"><span class=\"p-rid\">ADR-2</span><h4><code>engagement-kpi-map-versioning.md</code></h4><p>Schema-versioning convention (integer monotonic), changelog format, drift-detection CI check.</p></div>\n      </div>\n\n      <h3 class=\"sub\">Integration risks</h3>\n      <ul class=\"plain\">\n        <li><strong>Known:</strong> Confidence-cap interaction with existing MEDIUM caps from manual-input paste-ins — modifier never <em>raises</em> confidence; only caps at MEDIUM if not already capped.</li>\n        <li><strong>Known:</strong> Report-UI consumers need a new conditional render for the <code>engagement_modifier</code> block. UI sub-issue filed under ABA-66 before ship, with acceptance covering all four <code>status</code> values.</li>\n        <li><strong>Resolved by skeleton scope expansion:</strong> Extraction reliability on PDF/HTML/letter surfaces — all three in-scope, not just META.</li>\n        <li><strong>Resolved by backtest gate:</strong> YoY threshold calibration — per-ticker vs. global resolved from the 40 ticker-quarter sample before merge, not after.</li>\n      </ul>\n    </section>\n\n    <!-- 7. Sequencing -->\n    <section class=\"s\" id=\"sequencing\">\n      <header><span class=\"ix\">07</span><h2>Sequencing &amp; appetite</h2></header>\n      <p class=\"sec-intro\">Structured as a spike-then-build. Weeks 1–2 are a time-boxed spike whose deliverable is a PROCEED / KILL / RESHAPE decision. Week 3 only runs if Weeks 1–2 produce evidence. Total appetite: <strong>3 weeks from approval — fixed cap, not range.</strong> If a gate slips, the plan reshapes; it does not extend.</p>\n\n      <div class=\"p-seq\">\n        <div class=\"p-seq-row p-seq-row-head\">\n          <div>Week</div><div>Gate</div><div>Output</div><div>Slip behaviour</div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div class=\"p-seq-row\">\n          <div><span class=\"p-seq-week\">Week 1</span><br><span style=\"color:var(--muted); font-size:12.5px;\">Walking skeleton + feasibility</span></div>\n          <div>\n            (a) Three-surface skeleton (META PDF, RDDT HTML, NFLX letter) passes ≥ 80% precision (NFR2) and ±4% cap (NFR4).<br>\n            (b) Reproducibility — same-day same-ticker repeat produces identical values across 5 trials.<br>\n            (c) Backtest data-source feasibility — one historical ticker-quarter end-to-end.\n          </div>\n          <div>Spike branch with working GATHER on three tickers; precision + reproducibility fixtures committed; data-source decision documented.</div>\n          <div class=\"p-seq-slip\">If any surface fails after one prompt-iteration round → STOP. If reproducibility fails → STOP (defeats audit-trail contract). If no viable backtest data source → modifier ships disabled-by-default.</div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div class=\"p-seq-row\">\n          <div><span class=\"p-seq-week\">Week 2</span><br><span style=\"color:var(--muted); font-size:12.5px;\">ADR pre-reg → backtest → ADR finalise</span></div>\n          <div>\n            (a) Constants ADR pre-registered <em>before</em> backtest runs.<br>\n            (b) Backtest runs blind on the 40-candidate / 32-min / 24-floor sample.<br>\n            (c) ≥ 60% revision-direction agreement (NFR7).<br>\n            (d) Map-versioning ADR finalised.\n          </div>\n          <div>Backtest suite green; both ADRs merged.</div>\n          <div class=\"p-seq-slip\">If hit rate &lt; 60% → <strong>one</strong> constant revision allowed (documented as ADR supersession with explicit \"in-sample retune\"); a second failure ships disabled-by-default.</div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div class=\"p-seq-row\" style=\"background:var(--accent-soft);\">\n          <div><span class=\"p-seq-week\" style=\"color:var(--accent);\">Spike → Build gate</span><br><span style=\"color:var(--muted); font-size:12.5px;\">end of Week 2</span></div>\n          <div><strong>PROCEED</strong> if: all three surfaces ≥80% precision; reproducibility passes; backtest ≥60%; both ADRs merged.<br><strong>KILL</strong> if: precision fails on multiple surfaces with no fix; backtest materially &lt;60%; data source unobtainable.<br><strong>RESHAPE</strong> if: only one surface fails (drop ticker); or backtest passes on a different lever (revenue vs. margin) — re-author constants ADR and shift Week 3 scope.</div>\n          <div>Written go/no-go memo in <code>docs/design-docs/engagement-kpi-enrichment/spike-decision.md</code> summarising what was learned, evidence, and recommended path. Linked from ABA-66.</div>\n          <div>KILL closes ABA-66 with the memo as deliverable; PROCEED unlocks Week 3.</div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div class=\"p-seq-row\">\n          <div><span class=\"p-seq-week\">Week 3</span><br><span style=\"color:var(--muted); font-size:12.5px;\">Build (only if PROCEED)</span></div>\n          <div>Full skill changes merged; KPI-map v1 with <code>schema_version</code> + changelog; smoke + golden + backtest tests green in CI; UI sub-issue filed; weekly drift-CI workflow authored.</div>\n          <div>Feature ready to ship; ABA-66 closed.</div>\n          <div class=\"p-seq-slip\">If Week 3 slips, the half-built feature does not ship — revert the skeleton branch and re-scope.</div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </section>\n\n    <!-- 8. Operability -->\n    <section class=\"s\" id=\"operability\">\n      <header><span class=\"ix\">08</span><h2>Operability plan</h2></header>\n\n      <h3 class=\"sub\">Metrics (load-bearing only)</h3>\n      <ul class=\"plain\">\n        <li><code>engagement_modifier_extraction_precision</code> — golden-fixture pass rate (NFR2). Floor: 80%.</li>\n        <li><code>engagement_modifier_extraction_tokens{ticker}</code> — per-invocation in-session tokens (NFR6). Cap: 8k.</li>\n        <li><code>engagement_modifier_base_iv_impact_pct{ticker}</code> — IV delta per applied run (NFR4). Cap: 5%.</li>\n        <li><code>engagement_modifier_revision_agreement_rate</code> — rolling 8-quarter outcome metric (NFR7). Floor: 60%; OOS floor 55%.</li>\n        <li><code>engagement_modifier_kpi_map_drift_check</code> — weekly dry-run pass/fail per seed ticker.</li>\n      </ul>\n\n      <h3 class=\"sub\">Structured log (emitted at GATHER end)</h3>\n      <div class=\"p-code\">\n        <div class=\"p-code-file\">stdout · one line per invocation</div>\n        <pre class=\"p-code-body\">{<span class=\"tok-str\">\"event\"</span>: <span class=\"tok-str\">\"engagement_modifier\"</span>, <span class=\"tok-str\">\"ticker\"</span>: <span class=\"tok-str\">\"META\"</span>, <span class=\"tok-str\">\"ai_layer\"</span>: <span class=\"tok-str\">\"INCUMBENT\"</span>,\n <span class=\"tok-str\">\"status\"</span>: <span class=\"tok-str\">\"applied\"</span>, <span class=\"tok-str\">\"kpi_name\"</span>: <span class=\"tok-str\">\"DAP\"</span>, <span class=\"tok-str\">\"yoy\"</span>: <span class=\"tok-fn\">0.061</span>, <span class=\"tok-str\">\"direction\"</span>: <span class=\"tok-fn\">1</span>,\n <span class=\"tok-str\">\"magnitude\"</span>: <span class=\"tok-str\">\"mild\"</span>, <span class=\"tok-str\">\"y1_anchor_multiplier\"</span>: <span class=\"tok-fn\">1.02</span>,\n <span class=\"tok-str\">\"base_iv_impact_pct\"</span>: <span class=\"tok-fn\">0.018</span>, <span class=\"tok-str\">\"extraction_tokens\"</span>: <span class=\"tok-fn\">5200</span>,\n <span class=\"tok-str\">\"source_accession\"</span>: <span class=\"tok-str\">\"0001628280-26-028364\"</span>, <span class=\"tok-str\">\"source_type\"</span>: <span class=\"tok-str\">\"edgar_8k_exh99\"</span>}</pre>\n      </div>\n\n      <h3 class=\"sub\">Alerts (CI fitness functions that block merge)</h3>\n      <ul class=\"plain\">\n        <li>Smoke test fails (NFR1 latency, NFR2 precision, NFR4 cap) → CI red.</li>\n        <li>Golden-fixture precision &lt; 80% → CI red.</li>\n        <li>Token consumption &gt; 8k per invocation in smoke suite → CI red.</li>\n        <li><strong>Backtest revision-agreement &lt; 60%</strong> on rolling 8-quarter sample → CI red; modifier auto-disables until investigated.</li>\n        <li><strong>OOS monitor:</strong> running tally of fresh ticker-quarter outcomes after launch; floor 55% over trailing 8 fresh ticker-quarters → modifier auto-disables and surfaces a one-line notice.</li>\n        <li><strong>KPI-map drift CI</strong> (weekly): if <code>status != \"applied\"</code> for any seed ticker for &gt;2 consecutive runs, the check fails loudly. Surfaces map staleness within ~2 weeks of a publisher format change.</li>\n      </ul>\n\n      <h3 class=\"sub\">Rollback</h3>\n      <ul class=\"plain\">\n        <li><strong>Per-run:</strong> <code>/stock-model META --no-engagement-modifier</code> disables the GATHER step entirely.</li>\n        <li><strong>Skill-level:</strong> single-commit revert to the preceding skill version. ETA ~5 minutes.</li>\n        <li><strong>JSON contract:</strong> <code>engagement_modifier</code> block is optional with <code>status: \"unavailable\"</code> fallback. Removing the field does not break prior reports.</li>\n      </ul>\n\n      <h3 class=\"sub\">Known failure modes</h3>\n      <table class=\"p-fmtbl\">\n        <thead><tr><th>Failure mode</th><th>Mitigation</th></tr></thead>\n        <tbody>\n          <tr><td>Earnings release not yet published (mid-quarter run)</td><td>GATHER finds no fresh release; sets <code>status: \"unavailable\"</code>; skill proceeds without modifier.</td></tr>\n          <tr><td>Web search returns wrong URL (e.g. competitor's release)</td><td>LLM extraction verifies the ticker name appears in source. If not, <code>status: \"unavailable\"</code>.</td></tr>\n          <tr><td>LLM extracts wrong number</td><td>MIP gate surfaces number + source URL to user. User rejects → <code>status: \"user_skipped\"</code>.</td></tr>\n          <tr><td>KPI definition changed between quarters (e.g. DAU → DAP)</td><td>KPI-map maintenance task; until updated, extraction fail-softs to <code>\"unavailable\"</code>.</td></tr>\n          <tr><td>YoY data unavailable (first disclosure of new metric)</td><td>Computes QoQ if <code>comparison_basis: \"QoQ_fallback\"</code>; otherwise <code>\"unavailable\"</code>.</td></tr>\n          <tr><td>User runs <code>/stock-model</code> non-interactively (CI / batch)</td><td>MIP gate is interactive — non-interactive runs emit <code>status: \"unavailable\"</code>.</td></tr>\n        </tbody>\n      </table>\n    </section>\n\n    <!-- 9. Open questions -->\n    <section class=\"s\" id=\"open\">\n      <header><span class=\"ix\">09</span><h2>Open questions</h2></header>\n      <p class=\"sec-intro\">Five load-bearing OQs sequenced against the spike gates; OQ4 resolved in this revision; OQ7 filed separately as v2 follow-on.</p>\n      <div class=\"p-questions\">\n\n        <div class=\"p-q\">\n          <div class=\"p-q-title\"><span class=\"p-q-id\">OQ1</span>Lever — revenue, FCF margin, or both?</div>\n          <div class=\"p-q-body\">Revenue is the more direct link to engagement; margin captures monetisation.</div>\n          <div class=\"p-q-meta\"><span>owner: Anton</span><span>resolves: end of Week 1 (skeleton output)</span></div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div class=\"p-q\">\n          <div class=\"p-q-title\"><span class=\"p-q-id\">OQ2</span>YoY thresholds — per-ticker or global?</div>\n          <div class=\"p-q-body\">±2% deadband and 8% strong-magnitude — calibrate per-ticker, or single globals? RDDT and META have very different baseline growth rates.</div>\n          <div class=\"p-q-meta\"><span>owner: Anton</span><span>resolves: end of Week 2 (backtest data)</span></div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div class=\"p-q\">\n          <div class=\"p-q-title\"><span class=\"p-q-id\">OQ3</span>NFLX substitute KPI?</div>\n          <div class=\"p-q-body\">NFLX stopped publishing quarterly net-adds in Q1 2025. Drop NFLX from seed set if no substitute can be authored.</div>\n          <div class=\"p-q-meta\"><span>owner: Anton</span><span>resolves: start of Week 1 (KPI-map authoring)</span></div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div class=\"p-q p-q-resolved\">\n          <div class=\"p-q-title\"><span class=\"p-q-id p-q-id-resolved\">OQ4 · resolved</span>KPI-map maintenance cadence</div>\n          <div class=\"p-q-body\">Resolved: weekly drift-CI check (see Alerts) plus on-print ad-hoc review the Monday after each seed ticker's earnings date. Authored into <code>docs/adrs/engagement-kpi-map-versioning.md</code>.</div>\n          <div class=\"p-q-meta\"><span>owner: Anton</span><span>resolved: this revision</span></div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div class=\"p-q\">\n          <div class=\"p-q-title\"><span class=\"p-q-id\">OQ5</span>Bear/bull scenarios — apply or skip?</div>\n          <div class=\"p-q-body\">Modifier on bear/bull as well, or remain base-only? Locked into the constants ADR.</div>\n          <div class=\"p-q-meta\"><span>owner: Anton</span><span>resolves: end of Week 1</span></div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div class=\"p-q\">\n          <div class=\"p-q-title\"><span class=\"p-q-id\">OQ6</span>GOOGL fallback KPI?</div>\n          <div class=\"p-q-body\">INCUMBENT tickers without disclosed engagement KPIs (e.g. GOOGL) — fall back to \"paid clicks YoY %\" or skip? Same drop rule as NFLX if no defensible mapping.</div>\n          <div class=\"p-q-meta\"><span>owner: Anton</span><span>resolves: start of Week 1 (KPI-map authoring)</span></div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div class=\"p-q p-q-resolved\">\n          <div class=\"p-q-title\"><span class=\"p-q-id p-q-id-resolved\">OQ7 · deferred</span>KPI-discovery skill as v2 follow-on</div>\n          <div class=\"p-q-body\">Hard-coded map is right for v1's 4-ticker seed. If the monitored universe grows past ~8 tickers, manual map maintenance becomes the bottleneck. A separate skill (<code>/stock-kpi-discover TICKER</code>) could classify the business model and auto-propose a mapping for human confirmation. Filed as a separate Linear issue rather than absorbed into ABA-66 — scope is \"later\" by Now-Next-Later.</div>\n          <div class=\"p-q-meta\"><span>owner: Anton</span><span>status: filed separately</span></div>\n        </div>\n\n      </div>\n    </section>\n\n    <footer>\n      Rendered from <code>docs/design-docs/engagement-kpi-enrichment/design-doc.md</code> · 2026-05-17\n    </footer>\n\n  </main>\n</div>\n</body>\n</html>\n"
}
```

> AGENT

Design-doc rendered: `~/src/stock-review/docs/design-docs/engagement-kpi-enrichment/design-doc.html`. Exercises the full design-doc pattern surface:

- **Frontmatter → head** — H1 with monospace `/stock-model` chip in the title, lede, pill row (Accepted / Appetite·3w / scope / counts), meta-grid with linked plan-review + spike-decision
- **Acceptance-note callout** — warn-coloured `p-callout` at the top of the body, prominent enough that you can't miss the "lead → confirm/lag" pivot that supersedes parts of the body
- **Per-ticker reality-check table** — real `<table>` with the rebrand notes (DAU→DAP, etc.)
- **P12 rubric cards** — FR1–FR8 with accent ID pill, then NFR1–NFR7 with blue ID pill + dashed-rule **Fitness** footer line for each
- **Alt-card variant** — Alt A/C/D have red-left-border with ✗ chevron + "Rejected" pill; Alt B has green-left-border with ✓ chevron + "Chosen" pill + a subtle green→surface gradient fill. Blast-radius / reversal-cost / why-rejected as a compact `<dl>` inside each card
- **P14 SVG flow diagram** — GATHER step rendered as 8 nodes in two rows: Signal → AI-layer gate → KPI-map lookup → (down) EDGAR fetch → extract → MIP confirm → apply. Solid edges = happy path, dashed accent edges = skip/unavailable routes back to "status: unavailable". Inline `<svg>` with `<marker>` arrows, `role="img" aria-label="…"` for a11y
- **P16 code panels** — three of them: JSON contract, OUTPUT line, structured-log line. Token spans for kw/str/cm/fn
- **Sequencing table** — custom `p-seq` grid (Week / Gate / Output / Slip-behaviour). The "Spike → Build gate" row gets an accent-soft background and accent week label so it visually separates from the three weeks
- **Failure-modes table** — clean `<table>` for the 6 mitigations
- **P5 open-question cards** — OQ1–OQ7. OQ4 and OQ7 use the resolved variant (green left-border, green ID pill).

Open it and tell me what reads well / what's off. After that, task 10 is writing the SKILL.md itself.

> AGENT

Done. Summary of fixes applied to both files:

**Contrast (the actual AA failure):**
- Code-panel comment token `#6a7480` → `#a0aabb` on `#14141a` (3.5:1 → 7.0:1, AAA)
- Token panel keyword/function colors lightened slightly (`#c792ea` → `#d8a8ff`, `#82aaff` → `#9cc0ff`)
- Code-panel base text `#e8e6de` → `#ededf2` (already passed; tighter contrast for body code)
- Code-file caption color: muted → fg-soft
- `.sec-intro` color: muted → fg-soft (it's body intro text, shouldn't be the dimmest thing on the page)
- `.p-q-meta`, `.p-fitness`, `.p-diagram-caption`, `.p-edge-label`, `.p-node-sub`, footer, `.p-task-when`, `.p-task-deps`: muted → fg-soft

**Font sizes (AA doesn't mandate but practical floor of 12px for labels, 14px for body):**
- All 11/11.5px uppercase labels → 12px (kickers, status block, summary-k, callout-label, rid pills, table headers, tags, q-id, fitness footer, alt-tag, nav numerals, alt-rows dt)
- 13.25–13.75px body text → 14px (rubric body, alt-rows dd, alt p, q-body, seq-row, fmtbl, rtbl, task-ac, task-deps, meta-grid dd, breadcrumbs)
- 10.5–12.5px caption/diagram text → 11.5–13px
- 12.75px code panel body → 13px

The palette tokens themselves were already AA-compliant; the failures were concentrated in the dark code panel and the over-use of `--muted` on body text where `--fg-soft` was the right choice.

Reload and check — especially the code panels (comments in `// Emitted by every invocation…` line) and any text inside rubric cards or sequencing rows.

```
1	# Skill anatomy
2	
3	The authoring spec for `SKILL.md` files in `skills/<name>/`.
4	
5	## Frontmatter
6	
7	```yaml
8	---
9	name: <skill-name>
10	description: <1-2 sentences, includes trigger phrases>
11	pack: <product | engineering | meta>
12	lifecycle_stage: <define | plan | build | ship | verify | review | meta | reference>
13	principles_implemented:
14	  - source: <eng-universal | eng-agentic | roadmap | product>
15	    id: <principle or rule id>
16	    bucket: <embedded | standalone | hook | sub-agent>
17	length_target: <e.g. 200-280>
18	author: Anton Babushkin
19	predecessor:
20	  repo: <URL or "none">
21	  skill: <skill name in source repo, or "none">
22	  relation: <derivative | adjacent | new>
23	kept_from_predecessor: <list, or "n/a">
24	changed_from_predecessor: <list, or "n/a">
25	---
26	```
27	
28	## Sections
29	
30	In this exact order:
31	
32	1. **Title** — `# <Skill Name>`
33	2. **Purpose** — one paragraph
34	3. **When to use** — explicit trigger phrases and intents, with the size-the-work rubric where relevant
35	4. **When not to use** — explicit non-triggers, with the right alternative skill named
36	5. **Inputs** — what the skill expects to be given
37	6. **Outputs** — what it produces, including the artefact path under `docs/`
38	7. **Workflow** — numbered steps with hard gates marked `[GATE]`
39	8. **Artefact template** — the markdown the skill produces
40	9. **Common rationalisations** — table of typical excuses and rebuttals
41	10. **Red flags** — bullet list of signs the skill is being violated
42	11. **Verification / exit criteria** — how to know the skill ran correctly
43	12. **References** — pointers to `references/` and to source principles
44	
45	## Length
46	
47	Target 100–300 lines. Below 100, editor checks for under-specification (acceptable if genuinely complete). Above 300, editor checks for redundancy with other skills, scope creep, or over-explanation. Hard cap 350 lines; beyond that, split.
48	
49	## Artefact template section name
50	
51	The anatomy calls this section "Artefact template." Skills that produce a single named artefact may use a descriptive variant (e.g. "Task list template", "Finding template") — the name should identify the artefact type. The section must still appear in the required position.
52	
53	## Root rules files
54	
55	`PRODUCT_RULES.md`, `eng-principles-universal.md`, and `eng-principles-agentic.md` mirror the shape of their source principles documents, not this anatomy.
56	
57	## Meta-pack skills
58	
59	Skills with `pack: meta` are navigation and orientation artefacts, not workflow skills. They are exempt from: `principles_implemented` frontmatter, `predecessor` frontmatter, and the body sections Inputs, Outputs, Artefact template, Common rationalisations, Red flags, and Verification / exit criteria. They must still have: Title, Purpose, When to use, When not to use, and a References or equivalent lookup table.
60	
61	## See also
62	
63	- `docs/hook-anatomy.md` — for hooks
64	- `docs/sub-agent-anatomy.md` — for sub-agent personas
65	- `docs/brief-template.md` — for the brief that drives authoring
66	- `docs/authoring-learnings.md` — calibration guidance and failure modes
67	
```

1	---
2	name: render-html
3	description: >
4	  Convert a markdown design doc, plan, ADR, or roadmap into a single self-contained
5	  HTML file for human review. Use when an artefact is about to go to review or
6	  sign-off and the reviewer experience matters — markdown flattens spatial content
7	  (alternatives, dependency graphs, diffs, timelines) that HTML can present
8	  faithfully. Trigger phrases: "render this doc as HTML", "make this reviewable",
9	  "convert design doc to HTML", "I want to share this plan", "share this for
10	  review", "make a readable version". Use this skill whenever the user is about to
11	  hand a markdown artefact to a human reviewer, even if they don't explicitly say
12	  the word HTML.
13	pack: engineering
14	lifecycle_stage: review
15	principles_implemented:
16	  - source: eng-agentic

31	
32	# Render HTML
33	
34	## Purpose
35	
36	This skill converts a markdown artefact — design doc, task list, ADR, roadmap, postmortem — into one self-contained HTML file written next to the source as `<source>.html`. The deliverable is for humans about to review or sign off on the artefact, not for archival or publication. It is the operational form of agentic Principle 7 (memory lives in artefacts): the doc itself, made navigable and legible, is the persistent record reviewers and future agents read. And it is a lever on universal Principle 1 (shipped is not done; observed is done): better review UX raises the rate at which review actually happens, which raises the rate at which outcomes get observed.
37	
38	The v0.1 skill produces a clean walking-skeleton render — title, sticky TOC sidebar generated from H2/H3, prose body, code blocks, footer with provenance. Rich pattern-specific treatments (side-by-side alternatives, kanban roadmaps, diff blocks, dependency timelines) arrive in later iterations as the pattern catalogue is built out. The walking skeleton must work end-to-end on any structured markdown before any pattern logic is added.
39	
40	## When to use
41	
42	- The user asks to "render", "convert to HTML", "make this reviewable", or "share this doc" and points at a markdown file.
43	- A markdown artefact is about to go to a human reviewer for design review, code review prep, ADR sign-off, or stakeholder alignment.
44	- The doc is long enough that navigation matters (more than ~3 H2 sections) or contains structure that flattens badly in markdown previewers.
45	
46	## When not to use
47	
48	- **Drafting** — write the markdown first. HTML rendering is for finished or near-finished artefacts, not works in progress. The output is regenerated each time the source changes; rendering mid-draft burns cycles for no review benefit.
49	- **Single-screen documents** — a 30-line note doesn't earn the overhead of a TOC and shell. Send the markdown.
50	- **Documents the reviewer will edit** — HTML round-trip back to markdown is not supported. If reviewers want to suggest edits, send the markdown or use `/crit`.
51	- **Multi-document sites** — one source in, one file out. For a site, use a static-site generator.
52	
53	## Inputs
54	
55	- Path to a single `.md` file with at least one H1 and structured H2/H3 sections.
56	- Optional: prior `<source>.html` exists alongside the source (triggers overwrite confirmation).
57	
58	## Outputs
59	
60	- A single HTML file written to `<source>.html` — same directory as the source, same basename, `.html` extension.
61	- The file is self-contained: inline `<style>`, inline `<script>`, no `<link rel="stylesheet">`, no `<script src=>`, no remote images. Opens correctly via `file://` with the network offline.
62	- Size budget: ≤ ~300KB for typical inputs; hard cap 500KB.
63	
64	## Workflow
65	
66	**Step 1 — Source check [GATE]**
67	Read the source markdown. Confirm it has at least one H1 and at least one H2. If not, stop and report: "render-html expects structured markdown — source has no H1/H2." Do not invent structure to make the doc renderable. The skill is a renderer, not a restructurer.
68	
69	**Step 2 — Overwrite check [GATE]**
70	Compute the output path: replace the source's `.md` extension with `.html`. If a file already exists at that path, prompt the user for confirmation before proceeding: "`<output-path>` exists. Overwrite? (y/n)". Do not silently overwrite. Do not append timestamps or version suffixes — re-rendering must be idempotent so diffs are meaningful. If the user declines, stop.
71	
72	**Step 3 — Inventory headings**
73	Walk the markdown and collect every H2 and H3 in document order. Generate a stable slug for each (kebab-case from the heading text, deduplicated with `-2`, `-3` if needed). These become both anchor IDs in the body and the TOC entries in the sidebar. H1 is the document title — it goes in the page header, not the TOC.
74	
75	**Step 4 — Convert body**
76	Walk the markdown section by section (H2-delimited). For each section, read `references/html-patterns.md` and check the patterns in declared order — first match wins. If a pattern matches, emit its HTML template and append its CSS rules to the collected pattern-CSS buffer. If nothing matches, render the section as straightforward prose using standard CommonMark semantics (H2/H3 with anchor IDs from Step 3, paragraphs, lists, blockquotes, fenced code blocks preserving language hints, inline code, tables, links). Prose is the fallback, not the exception — false-positive pattern matches degrade reviewer trust faster than missing treatments.
77	
78	**Step 5 — Compose the page**
79	Read the HTML shell from `references/html-skeleton.md`. Substitute the tokens defined there: `{{title}}` (H1 from the source), `{{source-path}}`, `{{render-date}}` (ISO date only, no time, so same-day re-renders produce no diff), `{{toc-entries}}` (one `<li class="h2|h3">` per entry from Step 3), and `{{body-html}}` (the converted body from Step 4). Append the pattern-CSS buffer from Step 4 to the end of the inline `<style>` block — patterns add new selectors prefixed `p-`, never overriding the prose styles.
80	
81	**Step 6 — Self-containment check [GATE]**
82	Before writing the file, verify the composed HTML contains zero `<link rel="stylesheet">`, zero `<script src=>`, and no `<img src="http...">` or `<img src="//...">`. SVG and base64 data URIs are allowed; remote URLs are not. If any external reference is present, fix the shell rather than ship the violation.
83	
84	**Step 7 — Write and report**
85	Write the file to `<source>.html`. Report the output path, file size, and the count of H2/H3 entries in the TOC. Tell the user: "Open the file in your browser to verify TOC navigation and that no console errors appear." This handoff is required — the skill cannot observe the rendered page itself (universal Principle 1: the agent has shipped, the user observes).
86	
87	## Artefact template
88	
89	The HTML shell — `<!doctype html>` template, inline CSS, scrollspy JS, and the substitution-token contract (`{{title}}`, `{{source-path}}`, `{{render-date}}`, `{{toc-entries}}`, `{{body-html}}`) — lives in `references/html-skeleton.md`. Read that file in Step 5 and substitute the tokens. The shell is not duplicated here so a single edit to the skeleton updates both this skill and any future pattern-aware extensions of it.
90	
91	## Common rationalisations
92	
93	| Rationalisation | Rebuttal |
94	|---|---|
95	| "The markdown previewer is fine — they can just read it on GitHub." | GitHub flattens side-by-side alternatives into stacked blocks, hides long task lists in scroll, and gives no in-doc navigation for 40-section design docs. The reviewer experience is the bottleneck on outcome-observation (universal Principle 1); a renderer optimised for review removes it. |
96	| "I'll add a CDN link for the syntax highlighter / icon font / chart library." | The self-containment rule is load-bearing. Reviewers open these files via `file://`, attach them to emails, store them in private wikis. A broken CDN reference produces a silently-degraded page that the reviewer doesn't know to distrust. Inline or omit. |
97	| "Let me append a timestamp to the filename so I don't overwrite." | Idempotent re-rendering is the property that makes the output diffable and the source the canonical record. Versioned filenames produce orphaned copies. Prompt before overwriting (Step 2); do not invent new names. |
98	| "The source has no headings yet — let me invent some structure to make it renderable." | The skill is a renderer, not a restructurer. Inventing headings creates artefacts that look authoritative but were never agreed. If the source isn't structured, fix the source first. |
99	| "I'll skip the TOC for short docs to save space." | The TOC is the navigation contract reviewers learn to expect. Suppressing it inconsistently trains reviewers to scroll-hunt every doc. If a doc is short enough that the TOC is useless, the doc is short enough that the skill doesn't apply (see When not to use). |
100	
101	## Red flags
102	
103	- Output contains `<link rel="stylesheet">`, `<script src=>`, or remote `<img>` URLs.
104	- File written to a path other than `<source>.html` (no timestamps, no version suffixes, no other directories).
105	- Existing output silently overwritten without prompt.
106	- TOC entries do not match the H2/H3 headings in the body.
107	- Body markdown silently dropped (e.g., a fenced code block missing) rather than rendered.
108	- File size above 500KB.
109	- HTML claims to render structure not present in the source.
110	
111	## Verification / exit criteria
112	
113	The skill has run correctly when:
114	
115	1. `<source>.html` exists in the same directory as the source.
116	2. Opening the file via `file://` in a browser produces no console errors or warnings.
117	3. The DevTools Network panel shows zero network requests after load.
118	4. Every H2 and H3 in the source appears as a TOC entry and is reachable by clicking.
119	5. The page renders correctly with the network disabled.
120	6. File size is below 500KB.
121	7. Re-running the skill against the same source (after declining overwrite) leaves the existing file untouched; accepting overwrite produces an HTML whose diff against the previous render is limited to the provenance line.
122	8. The user has been told to open the file and confirm — the skill has not declared the render verified on its own (universal Principle 1).
123	
124	## References
125	
126	- `references/html-skeleton.md` — the self-contained HTML shell, substitution tokens, and the hard self-containment rule
127	- `references/html-patterns.md` — the pattern catalogue (alternatives, timelines, callouts, kanban, diffs, chips) consulted in Step 4
128	- `rules/eng-principles-agentic.md` — P7 (memory lives in artefacts)
129	- `rules/eng-principles-universal.md` — P1 (shipped is not done; observed is done)
130	- Thariq Shihipar — "HTML effectiveness" (https://thariqs.github.io/html-effectiveness/) — case for HTML as a substrate for spatial review content
131	- `docs/skill-anatomy.md` — frontmatter and section-order contract
132	- `docs/tasks/render-html.md` — task plan; later iterations build the pattern catalogue at `references/html-patterns.md`
133	
```
